# ex_14 -- Supplementary Table S10 (Section S10: HDF vs graph neural networks; reviewer comment R2.1)
# Paper: Supplementary Section S10, Table S10 (MLP: HDF vs random GIN/GATv2 encoders vs trained GIN/GATv2);
#        hyperparameters in Supplementary Table S15. The HDF column (MLP) is reused by Table S11 (ex_19) and by ex_26.
#        The KNN comparison (gnn_comparison_knn) is computed by the same runs but is not printed in the SI.
# Source: graph_hdc revision/consolidated (9eaaa27), regenerated with _slurm_ex_14.py (full mode) in a scratch copy;
#         prefix ex_14_gnn (warmup ex_14_warmup). Identical to the used command files of the graph_hdc main checkout
#         (_ex14/warmup.txt, hdf.txt, gnn.txt of 2026-10-09) except the 50 hopv15_pce lines, whose TARGET_INDEX is
#         3 (real PCE) instead of 5 (a band gap): those rows were re-run on 2026-10-13 under the same prefix
#         (_slurm_fix_targets.py, KCIST job 51196), and the analysis skips the old index (target_matches).
#         Round reported: HDF arm of round 4 (2026-10-09, SPECTRUM unit) + GNN runs of round 3 (2026-10-08).
# Runs: 457 = warmup 7 + hdf_primer 70 + hdf 20 + gnn 360 (180 trained = 9 targets x 2 archs x 10 seeds; 180 random = 9 x 2 x 10)
# Protocol: 9 targets (FreeSolv, AqSolDB, Lipophilicity, BACE pIC50, HOPV15 PCE, COMPAS-3X gap, QM9 gap/U0/ZPVE),
#   seeds 0-9 (10% test; split drawn with the seed from the cached load__ dataset order).
#   HDF: D 2048, L 2, continuous encoding, unit-modulus codebooks (SPECTRUM unit), bidirectional, total H; MLP (100,100)
#   lr 1e-3 + KNN k=5 Euclidean. Random GNN: GIN/GATv2 with frozen random weights (width 2048, 2 layers, sum pooling),
#   same MLP/KNN. Trained GNN: 3x128 conv, head 128-64-32, batch 32, lr 1e-4 cosine to 1e-6, 1000 epochs, best-val
#   epoch restored. All GNNs get the HDF atom features (NODE_FEATURES hdf, total H).
# Stages: warmup builds the shared load__/stats_ caches (must finish first). hdf_primer: one HDF run per (dataset,
#   seed), which writes the HDF encoding cache that the other QM9 targets of that seed reuse (hdf must wait for it).
#   gnn needs only the warmup (could run in parallel with the hdf stages).
# Analysis: python analyze_ex_14.py   (-> _ex14/results_ex_14_gnn.csv, gnn_comparison_mlp_ex_14_gnn.tex = Table S10,
#   gnn_comparison_knn_ex_14_gnn.tex, gnn_comparison_cost_ex_14_gnn.tex, gnn_convergence_ex_14_gnn.txt)
# Cost: trained GNNs ~700 GPU-h in total (RTX 4090 on KCIST, 3 runs per GPU; a trained QM9 run takes ~8-12 h, COMPAS
#   ~4.5 h); random GNNs ~1 GPU-h; HDF ~10-15 CPU-h (a QM9 / COMPAS-3X encoding ~18-21 min per seed, cached).
#   Random GNNs use DEVICE cuda (the runner may swap in cpu); trained GNNs pick the GPU automatically.
# Notes: datasets come from chem_mat_data (network access for qm9_smiles, compas_3x). The run in the paper used
#   run_ex14_kcist.sbatch (TMPDIR per job, OMP threads = CPUs / 12 workers); only the python lines are kept here.
#   ex_19 and ex_26 depend on this experiment (same .cache folder -> same splits; analyze_ex_19 / analyze_ex_26 read
#   the ex_14 HDF archives from results/).
# @stage warmup
python predict_molecules__hdc.py --__DEBUG__="False" --__PREFIX__="'ex_14_warmup'" --SEED="420" --NUM_TEST="0.1" --NUM_TRAIN="1.0" --NUM_DATA="1.0" --DATASET_NAME="'freesolv'" --DATASET_TYPE="'regression'" --NOTE="'freesolv_hfe'" --NN_NUM_WORKERS="0" --TARGET_INDEX="0" --EMBEDDING_SIZE="256" --NUM_LAYERS="1" --DEVICE="'cpu'" --MODELS="['linear']" --BIDIRECTIONAL="True" --HYDROGEN_COUNT="'total'" --SPECTRUM="'gaussian'"
python predict_molecules__hdc.py --__DEBUG__="False" --__PREFIX__="'ex_14_warmup'" --SEED="420" --NUM_TEST="0.1" --NUM_TRAIN="1.0" --NUM_DATA="1.0" --DATASET_NAME="'aqsoldb'" --DATASET_TYPE="'regression'" --NOTE="'aqsoldb_logs'" --NN_NUM_WORKERS="0" --EMBEDDING_SIZE="256" --NUM_LAYERS="1" --DEVICE="'cpu'" --MODELS="['linear']" --BIDIRECTIONAL="True" --HYDROGEN_COUNT="'total'" --SPECTRUM="'gaussian'"
